Web Engineering (ASP.NET Core) Assignment
Assignment: Course Management System API
This assignment focuses on implementing database services, dependency injection, entity
relationships, DTO validation, authentication, authorization, and optimized data querying using
LINQ in ASP.NET Core with Entity Framework Core.
Important Note About Project Models
You are NOT required to use the exact models described in this assignment. You may design your
own models and system idea as long as your project follows a similar structure and includes the
same technical requirements. This assignment is intended to serve as the foundation for your final
course project, so it is recommended that you design models and a system that you plan to
continue developing later in the semester.
Learning Objectives
•
Implement entity relationships in Entity Framework Core.
•
Use dependency injection with database services.
•
Design clean service layers.
•
Use DTOs for request and response models.
•
Validate incoming requests using DTO validation.
•
Implement JWT authentication.
•
Protect endpoints using authorization.
•
Optimize queries using LINQ Select projections.
•
Use AsNoTracking() for read-only queries.
Example Core Entities (Optional)
Entity
Description
Instructor
Represents a teacher responsible for courses
Student
Represents a student enrolled in courses
Course
Represents a university course
InstructorProfile
Stores additional information about an instructor
Enrollment
Represents student enrollment in a course
Required Relationships
•
Implement one-to-one relationship between two entities.

•
Implement one-to-many relationship between two entities.
•
Implement many-to-many relationship between two entities.
Required Services
•
Create at least three services that manage your main entities.
•
Services must use dependency injection for the database context.
DTO Requirements
•
Create DTOs for Create operations.
•
Create DTOs for Update operations.
•
Create DTOs for Read responses.
•
Controllers must NOT return entity models directly.
DTO Validation Requirements
•
Use Data Annotation attributes such as Required, MaxLength, MinLength, EmailAddress, and
Range.
•
Invalid requests must return HTTP 400 responses.
•
Validation should occur before performing database operations.
Authentication Requirements
•
Implement JWT authentication.
•
Create an authentication controller.
•
Provide a login endpoint that returns a JWT token.
•
Clients must send the token using the Authorization header.
Authorization Requirements
•
Protect API endpoints using the Authorize attribute.
•
Implement role-based authorization.
•
Example roles may include Admin, Instructor, or User.
•
Restrict certain endpoints to specific roles.
LINQ Optimization Requirements
•
Use Select() to project entities into DTOs.
•
Avoid returning entire entities when only partial data is required.

AsNoTracking Requirement
All read-only queries should use AsNoTracking() to improve performance.
Technical Requirements
•
Use ASP.NET Core Web API.
•
Use Entity Framework Core.
•
Use async database calls such as ToListAsync, FirstOrDefaultAsync, and SaveChangesAsync.
•
Create and apply EF Core migrations.
•
Document endpoints using Swagger.
Bonus (Optional)
•
Add a background cron job using Hangfire.
•
The job may perform a scheduled task such as cleaning old records, sending reminders, or
generating reports.
•
Implement refresh tokens for authentication to allow secure session renewal.
Submission Requirements
•
Complete source code.
•
Database migrations.
•
A README file explaining how to run the project.
•
The README must include all technologies used in the project with a short description of each.
•
The README must also explain why HTTP-only cookies are commonly used as an industry
standard for authentication security.
•
API endpoint documentation.
•
Screenshots from Swagger or Postman demonstrating working endpoints.

### Requirements Fulfilled:

- ✅ **LINQ Optimizations**: Select() projections, AsNoTracking()
- ✅ **Async Operations**: ToListAsync, FirstOrDefaultAsync, SaveChangesAsync
- ✅ **EF Core Migrations**: Created and applied
- ✅ **Swagger Documentation**: Configured with JWT auth support
- ✅ **README**: Technologies, HTTP-only cookies explanation

### 👤 User

how should the project work ?

---

<sub>⏱️ 114m 27s</sub>

### 💬 Copilot

## How the Course Management API Works

### 1. **Start the API**
```bash
cd CourseManagementAPI
dotnet run
```
Then open **http://localhost:5238/swagger** in your browser.

### 2. **Authentication Flow**
```
┌─────────────┐     POST /api/auth/login      ┌─────────────┐
│   Client    │ ─────────────────────────────→│    API      │
│             │ { username, password }        │             │
│             │ ←─────────────────────────────│             │
│             │      { JWT token }            │             │
└─────────────┘                               └─────────────┘
```

1. **Login** with `admin` / `Admin@123`
2. **Copy the JWT token** from response
3. **Add to requests**: `Authorization: Bearer \<token\>`

### 3. **Typical Usage Flow**

| Step | Endpoint | Role Required | Description |
|------|----------|---------------|-------------|
| 1 | `POST /api/auth/login` | None | Get JWT token |
| 2 | `POST /api/instructors` | Admin | Create instructors |
| 3 | `POST /api/courses` | Admin/Instructor | Create courses |
| 4 | `POST /api/students` | Admin | Create students |
| 5 | `POST /api/enrollments` | Admin/Student | Enroll students in courses |
| 6 | `PUT /api/enrollments/{id}` | Instructor | Assign grades |

### 4. **Testing in Swagger UI**

1. Click **"Authorize"** button (top-right 🔓)
2. Enter: `Bearer \<your-token\>`
3. Test any endpoint!

### 5. **Role Permissions**

| Role | Can Do |
|------|--------|
| **Admin** | Everything |
| **Instructor** | Manage courses, view students, grade |
| **Student** | View courses, enroll/unenroll |

### 6. **Example API Calls**

**Login:**
```json
POST /api/auth/login
{ "username": "admin", "password": "Admin@123" }
```

**Create Course:**
```json
POST /api/courses
{
  "courseCode": "CS101",
  "title": "Intro to Programming",
  "credits": 3,
  "semester": "Fall 2026",
  "startDate": "2026-09-01",
  "endDate": "2026-12-15"
}
```

**Enroll Student:**
```json
POST /api/enrollments
{ "studentId": 1, "courseId": 1 }
```

<sub>⏱️ 128m 21s</sub>

### 💬 Copilot

Great question! Let me explain:

## Role Capabilities

| Action | Admin | Instructor | Student |
|--------|:-----:|:----------:|:-------:|
| Create/Delete Instructors | ✅ | ❌ | ❌ |
| Create/Delete Students | ✅ | ❌ | ❌ |
| Create/Delete Courses | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ |
| View All Students | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ |
| Assign Grades | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ |
| Enroll in Courses | ✅ | ❌ | ✅ |
| View Courses | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |

## How to Create Users with Different Roles

**Step 1: Register new users** via `POST /api/auth/register`:

```json
// Create an Instructor user
{
  "username": "john_instructor",
  "email": "john@university.edu",
  "password": "Instructor@123",
  "role": "Instructor"
}

// Create a Student user
{
  "username": "alice_student", 
  "email": "alice@student.edu",
  "password": "Student@123",
  "role": "Student"
}
```

**Step 2:** Then login with those credentials to get their token.

## How to Create Instructor/Student Records

As **Admin**, create the actual records:

**Create Instructor** → `POST /api/instructors`:
```json
{
  "firstName": "John",
  "lastName": "Smith",
  "email": "john@university.edu",
  "department": "Computer Science"
}
```

**Create Student** → `POST /api/students`:
```json
{
  "firstName": "Alice",
  "lastName": "Johnson",
  "email": "alice@student.edu",
  "studentNumber": "STU001",
  "major": "Computer Science"
}
```

## Quick Test Flow

1. **As Admin** → Create instructor record, create student record, create course
2. **Register** a Student user → Login as Student → Enroll in course
3. **Register** an Instructor user → Login as Instructor → Assign grades

1. using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

3. 
4. namespace CourseManagementAPI.Models;
5. 
6. public class Enrollment
7. {
8.     public int Id { get; set; }
9.     
10.     // Foreign Keys for Many-to-Many relationship
11.     public int StudentId { get; set; }
12.     
13.     [ForeignKey("StudentId")]

18.     [ForeignKey("CourseId")]
19.     public Course Course { get; set; } = null!;
20.     
21.     public DateTime EnrollmentDate { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow;

28.     
29.     [MaxLength(5)]

33. }

51.                     Id = e.Course.Id,
52.                     CourseCode = e.Course.CourseCode,
53.                     Title = e.Course.Title,
54.                     Credits = e.Course.Credits,
55.                     Semester = e.Course.Semester
56.                 }
57.             })
58.             .ToListAsync();
59.     }
60. 
61.     public async Task<EnrollmentReadDto?> GetByIdAsync(int id)
62.     {
63.         return await _context.Enrollments
64.             .AsNoTracking()
65.             .Where(e => e.Id == id)
66.             .Select(e => new EnrollmentReadDto
67.             {
68.                 Id = e.Id,
69.                 StudentId = e.StudentId,
70.                 CourseId = e.CourseId,
71.                 EnrollmentDate = e.EnrollmentDate,
72.                 Status = e.Status,
73.                 Grade = e.Grade,
74.                 LetterGrade = e.LetterGrade,
75.                 CompletionDate = e.CompletionDate,
76.                 Student = new StudentSummaryDto
77.                 {
78.                     Id = e.Student.Id,
79.                     FullName = e.Student.FirstName + " " + e.Student.LastName,
80.                     StudentNumber = e.Student.StudentNumber,
81.                     Email = e.Student.Email
82.                 },
83.                 Course = new CourseSummaryDto
84.                 {
85.                     Id = e.Course.Id,
86.                     CourseCode = e.Course.CourseCode,
87.                     Title = e.Course.Title,
88.                     Credits = e.Course.Credits,
89.                     Semester = e.Course.Semester
90.                 }
91.             })
92.             .FirstOrDefaultAsync();
93.     }
94. 
95.     public async Task<EnrollmentReadDto?> CreateAsync(EnrollmentCreateDto dto)
96.     {
97.         // Check if student exists
98.         var studentExists = await _context.Students.AnyAsync(s => s.Id == dto.StudentId);
99.         if (!studentExists) return null;
100. 
101.         // Check if course exists
102.         var course = await _context.Courses
103.             .AsNoTracking()
104.             .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Id == dto.CourseId);
105.         if (course == null) return null;
106. 
107.         // Check if already enrolled
108.         var alreadyEnrolled = await IsStudentEnrolledAsync(dto.StudentId, dto.CourseId);
109.         if (alreadyEnrolled) return null;
110. 
111.         // Check if course has space
112.         var currentEnrollment = await _context.Enrollments
113.             .CountAsync(e => e.CourseId == dto.CourseId && e.Status == "Enrolled");
114.         if (currentEnrollment >= course.MaxEnrollment) return null;
115. 
116.         var enrollment = new Enrollment
117.         {
118.             StudentId = dto.StudentId,
119.             CourseId = dto.CourseId,
120.             EnrollmentDate = DateTime.UtcNow,
121.             Status = "Enrolled"
122.         };
123. 
124.         _context.Enrollments.Add(enrollment);
125.         await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
126. 
127.         return await GetByIdAsync(enrollment.Id);
128.     }
129. 
130.     public async Task<EnrollmentReadDto?> UpdateAsync(int id, EnrollmentUpdateDto dto)
131.     {
132.         var enrollment = await _context.Enrollments.FindAsync(id);
133.         if (enrollment == null) return null;
134. 
135.         if (dto.Status != null) enrollment.Status = dto.Status;
136.         if (dto.Grade.HasValue) enrollment.Grade = dto.Grade;
137.         if (dto.LetterGrade != null) enrollment.LetterGrade = dto.LetterGrade;
138.         if (dto.CompletionDate.HasValue) enrollment.CompletionDate = dto.CompletionDate;
139. 
140.         // Auto-set completion date when status is Completed
141.         if (dto.Status == "Completed" && !enrollment.CompletionDate.HasValue)
142.         {
143.             enrollment.CompletionDate = DateTime.UtcNow;
144.         }
145. 
146.         await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
147. 
148.         return await GetByIdAsync(id);
149.     }
150. 
151.     public async Task<bool> DeleteAsync(int id)
152.     {
153.         var enrollment = await _context.Enrollments.FindAsync(id);
154.         if (enrollment == null) return false;
155. 
156.         _context.Enrollments.Remove(enrollment);
157.         await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
158.         return true;
159.     }
160. 
161.     public async Task<bool> IsStudentEnrolledAsync(int studentId, int courseId)
162.     {
163.         return await _context.Enrollments
164.             .AsNoTracking()
165.             .AnyAsync(e => e.StudentId == studentId && e.CourseId == courseId);
166.     }
167. }
168.
```

1. using System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt;
2. using System.Security.Claims;
3. using System.Text;
4. using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
5. using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
6. using CourseManagementAPI.Data;
7. using CourseManagementAPI.DTOs;
8. using CourseManagementAPI.Models;
9. 
10. namespace CourseManagementAPI.Services;
11. 
12. public interface IAuthService
13. {
14.     Task<LoginResponseDto?> LoginAsync(LoginDto dto);
15.     Task<UserReadDto?> RegisterAsync(UserCreateDto dto);
16.     Task<UserReadDto?> GetUserByIdAsync(int id);
17. }
18. 
19. public class AuthService : IAuthService
20. {
21.     private readonly ApplicationDbContext _context;
22.     private readonly IConfiguration _configuration;
23. 
24.     public AuthService(ApplicationDbContext context, IConfiguration configuration)
25.     {
26.         _context = context;
27.         _configuration = configuration;
28.     }
29. 
30.     public async Task<LoginResponseDto?> LoginAsync(LoginDto dto)
31.     {
32.         var user = await _context.Users
33.             .FirstOrDefaultAsync(u => u.Username == dto.Username);
34. 
35.         if (user == null || !BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.Verify(dto.Password, user.PasswordHash))
36.         {
37.             return null;
38.         }
39. 
40.         // Update last login
41.         user.LastLoginAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
42.         await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
43. 
44.         var token = GenerateJwtToken(user);
45.         var expiration = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(
46.             double.Parse(_configuration["Jwt:ExpirationHours"] ?? "24"));
47. 
48.         return new LoginResponseDto
49.         {
50.             Token = token,
51.             Expiration = expiration,
52.             User = new UserReadDto
53.             {
54.                 Id = user.Id,
55.                 Username = user.Username,
56.                 Email = user.Email,
57.                 Role = user.Role,
58.                 CreatedAt = user.CreatedAt,
59.                 LastLoginAt = user.LastLoginAt
60.             }
61.         };
62.     }
63. 
64.     public async Task<UserReadDto?> RegisterAsync(UserCreateDto dto)
65.     {
66.         // Check if username or email already exists
67.         var exists = await _context.Users
68.             .AnyAsync(u => u.Username == dto.Username || u.Email == dto.Email);
69.         
70.         if (exists) return null;
71. 
72.         var user = new User
73.         {
74.             Username = dto.Username,
75.             Email = dto.Email,
76.             PasswordHash = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.HashPassword(dto.Password),
77.             Role = dto.Role,
78.             CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow
79.         };
80. 
81.         _context.Users.Add(user);
82.         await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
83. 
84.         return new UserReadDto
85.         {
86.             Id = user.Id,
87.             Username = user.Username,
88.             Email = user.Email,
89.             Role = user.Role,
90.             CreatedAt = user.CreatedAt
91.         };
92.     }
93. 
94.     public async Task<UserReadDto?> GetUserByIdAsync(int id)
95.     {
96.         return await _context.Users
97.             .AsNoTracking()
98.             .Where(u => u.Id == id)
99.             .Select(u => new UserReadDto
100.             {
101.                 Id = u.Id,
102.                 Username = u.Username,
103.                 Email = u.Email,
104.                 Role = u.Role,
105.                 CreatedAt = u.CreatedAt,
106.                 LastLoginAt = u.LastLoginAt
107.             })
108.             .FirstOrDefaultAsync();
109.     }
110. 
111.     private string GenerateJwtToken(User user)
112.     {
113.         var jwtKey = _configuration["Jwt:Key"] ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("JWT Key not configured");
114.         var key = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(jwtKey));
115.         var credentials = new SigningCredentials(key, SecurityAlgorithms.HmacSha256);
116. 
117.         var claims = new[]
118.         {
119.             new Claim(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier, user.Id.ToString()),
120.             new Claim(ClaimTypes.Name, user.Username),
121.             new Claim(ClaimTypes.Email, user.Email),
122.             new Claim(ClaimTypes.Role, user.Role)
123.         };
124. 
125.         var token = new JwtSecurityToken(
126.             issuer: _configuration["Jwt:Issuer"],
127.             audience: _configuration["Jwt:Audience"],
128.             claims: claims,
129.             expires: DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(
130.                 double.Parse(_configuration["Jwt:ExpirationHours"] ?? "24")),
131.             signingCredentials: credentials
132.         );
133. 
134.         return new JwtSecurityTokenHandler().WriteToken(token);
135.     }
136. }
137.
```

</details>

1. using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
2. using CourseManagementAPI.DTOs;
3. using CourseManagementAPI.Services;
4. 
5. namespace CourseManagementAPI.Controllers;
6. 
7. [ApiController]
8. [Route("api/[controller]")]
9. [Produces("application/json")]
10. public class AuthController : ControllerBase
11. {
12.     private readonly IAuthService _authService;
13. 
14.     public AuthController(IAuthService authService)
15.     {
16.         _authService = authService;
17.     }
18. 
19.     /// <summary>
20.     /// Login with username and password
21.     /// </summary>
22.     /// <param name="dto">Login credentials</param>
23.     /// <returns>JWT token and user info</returns>
24.     [HttpPost("login")]
25.     [ProducesResponseType(typeof(LoginResponseDto), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
26.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized)]
27.     public async Task<ActionResult<LoginResponseDto>> Login([FromBody] LoginDto dto)
28.     {
29.         var result = await _authService.LoginAsync(dto);
30.         
31.         if (result == null)
32.         {
33.             return Unauthorized(new { message = "Invalid username or password" });
34.         }
35. 
36.         return Ok(result);
37.     }
38. 
39.     /// <summary>
40.     /// Register a new user
41.     /// </summary>
42.     /// <param name="dto">User registration data</param>
43.     /// <returns>Created user info</returns>
44.     [HttpPost("register")]
45.     [ProducesResponseType(typeof(UserReadDto), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
46.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
47.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
48.     public async Task<ActionResult<UserReadDto>> Register([FromBody] UserCreateDto dto)
49.     {
50.         var result = await _authService.RegisterAsync(dto);
51.         
52.         if (result == null)
53.         {
54.             return Conflict(new { message = "Username or email already exists" });
55.         }
56. 
57.         return CreatedAtAction(nameof(Register), new { id = result.Id }, result);
58.     }
59. }
60. 
```

1. using System.Text;
2. using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer;
3. using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
4. using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
5. using Microsoft.OpenApi.Models;
6. using CourseManagementAPI.Data;
7. using CourseManagementAPI.Services;
8. 
9. var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);
10. 
11. // Add services to the container.
12. 
13. // Database Context - Using SQLite for easier setup
14. builder.Services.AddDbContext<ApplicationDbContext>(options =>
15.     options.UseSqlite(builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnection")));
16. 
17. // Register services with dependency injection
18. builder.Services.AddScoped<IAuthService, AuthService>();
19. builder.Services.AddScoped<IInstructorService, InstructorService>();
20. builder.Services.AddScoped<IStudentService, StudentService>();
21. builder.Services.AddScoped<ICourseService, CourseService>();
22. builder.Services.AddScoped<IEnrollmentService, EnrollmentService>();
23. 
24. // JWT Authentication
25. var jwtKey = builder.Configuration["Jwt:Key"] ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("JWT Key not configured");
26. builder.Services.AddAuthentication(options =>
27. {
28.     options.DefaultAuthenticateScheme = JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme;
29.     options.DefaultChallengeScheme = JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme;
30. })
31. .AddJwtBearer(options =>
32. {
33.     options.TokenValidationParameters = new TokenValidationParameters
34.     {
35.         ValidateIssuer = true,
36.         ValidateAudience = true,
37.         ValidateLifetime = true,
38.         ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,
39.         ValidIssuer = builder.Configuration["Jwt:Issuer"],
40.         ValidAudience = builder.Configuration["Jwt:Audience"],
41.         IssuerSigningKey = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(jwtKey))
42.     };
43. });
44. 
45. builder.Services.AddAuthorization();
46. 
47. builder.Services.AddControllers();
48. 
49. // Swagger/OpenAPI configuration
50. builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();
51. builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen(options =>
52. {
53.     options.SwaggerDoc("v1", new OpenApiInfo
54.     {
55.         Title = "Course Management API",
56.         Version = "v1",
57.         Description = "ASP.NET Core Web API for managing courses, instructors, students, and enrollments",
58.         Contact = new OpenApiContact
59.         {
60.             Name = "Course Management Team"
61.         }
62.     });
63. 
64.     // Add JWT authentication to Swagger
65.     options.AddSecurityDefinition("Bearer", new OpenApiSecurityScheme
66.     {
67.         Description = "JWT Authorization header using the Bearer scheme. Enter 'Bearer' [space] and then your token.",
68.         Name = "Authorization",
69.         In = ParameterLocation.Header,
70.         Type = SecuritySchemeType.ApiKey,
71.         Scheme = "Bearer"
72.     });
73. 
74.     options.AddSecurityRequirement(new OpenApiSecurityRequirement
75.     {
76.         {
77.             new OpenApiSecurityScheme
78.             {
79.                 Reference = new OpenApiReference
80.                 {
81.                     Type = ReferenceType.SecurityScheme,
82.                     Id = "Bearer"
83.                 }
84.             },
85.             Array.Empty<string>()
86.         }
87.     });
88. });
89. 
90. var app = builder.Build();
91. 
92. // Configure the HTTP request pipeline.
93. if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
94. {
95.     app.UseSwagger();
96.     app.UseSwaggerUI(options =>
97.     {
98.         options.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/v1/swagger.json", "Course Management API v1");
99.     });
100. }
101. 
102. app.UseAuthentication();
103. app.UseAuthorization();
104. 
105. app.MapControllers();
106. 
107. // Apply migrations automatically in development
108. using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
109. {
110.     var context = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<ApplicationDbContext>();
111.     context.Database.Migrate();
112. }
113. 
114. app.Run();
115.
```

```
1. using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
2. using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
3. using CourseManagementAPI.DTOs;
4. using CourseManagementAPI.Services;
5. 
6. namespace CourseManagementAPI.Controllers;
7. 
8. [ApiController]
9. [Route("api/[controller]")]
10. [Produces("application/json")]
11. [Authorize]
12. public class EnrollmentsController : ControllerBase
13. {
14.     private readonly IEnrollmentService _enrollmentService;
15. 
16.     public EnrollmentsController(IEnrollmentService enrollmentService)
17.     {
18.         _enrollmentService = enrollmentService;
19.     }
20. 
21.     /// <summary>
22.     /// Get all enrollments
23.     /// </summary>
24.     /// <returns>List of enrollments</returns>
25.     [HttpGet]
26.     [Authorize(Roles = "Admin,Instructor")]
27.     [ProducesResponseType(typeof(IEnumerable<EnrollmentReadDto>), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
28.     public async Task<ActionResult<IEnumerable<EnrollmentReadDto>>> GetAll()
29.     {
30.         var enrollments = await _enrollmentService.GetAllAsync();
31.         return Ok(enrollments);
32.     }
33. 
34.     /// <summary>
35.     /// Get an enrollment by ID
36.     /// </summary>
37.     /// <param name="id">Enrollment ID</param>
38.     /// <returns>Enrollment details</returns>
39.     [HttpGet("{id}")]
40.     [ProducesResponseType(typeof(EnrollmentReadDto), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
41.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
42.     public async Task<ActionResult<EnrollmentReadDto>> GetById(int id)
43.     {
44.         var enrollment = await _enrollmentService.GetByIdAsync(id);
45.         
46.         if (enrollment == null)
47.         {
48.             return NotFound(new { message = $"Enrollment with ID {id} not found" });
49.         }
50. 
51.         return Ok(enrollment);
52.     }
53. 
54.     /// <summary>
55.     /// Enroll a student in a course
56.     /// </summary>
57.     /// <param name="dto">Enrollment data</param>
58.     /// <returns>Created enrollment</returns>
59.     [HttpPost]
60.     [Authorize(Roles = "Admin,Student")]
61.     [ProducesResponseType(typeof(EnrollmentReadDto), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
62.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
63.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
64.     public async Task<ActionResult<EnrollmentReadDto>> Create([FromBody] EnrollmentCreateDto dto)
65.     {
66.         // Check if already enrolled
67.         var isEnrolled = await _enrollmentService.IsStudentEnrolledAsync(dto.StudentId, dto.CourseId);
68.         if (isEnrolled)
69.         {
70.             return Conflict(new { message = "Student is already enrolled in this course" });
71.         }
72. 
73.         var enrollment = await _enrollmentService.CreateAsync(dto);
74.         
75.         if (enrollment == null)
76.         {
77.             return BadRequest(new { message = "Unable to create enrollment. Please verify student and course exist and course is not full." });
78.         }
79. 
80.         return CreatedAtAction(nameof(GetById), new { id = enrollment.Id }, enrollment);
81.     }
82. 
83.     /// <summary>
84.     /// Update an enrollment (e.g., grades, status)
85.     /// </summary>
86.     /// <param name="id">Enrollment ID</param>
87.     /// <param name="dto">Updated enrollment data</param>
88.     /// <returns>Updated enrollment</returns>
89.     [HttpPut("{id}")]
90.     [Authorize(Roles = "Admin,Instructor")]
91.     [ProducesResponseType(typeof(EnrollmentReadDto), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
92.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
93.     public async Task<ActionResult<EnrollmentReadDto>> Update(int id, [FromBody] EnrollmentUpdateDto dto)
94.     {
95.         var enrollment = await _enrollmentService.UpdateAsync(id, dto);
96.         
97.         if (enrollment == null)
98.         {
99.             return NotFound(new { message = $"Enrollment with ID {id} not found" });
100.         }
101. 
102.         return Ok(enrollment);
103.     }
104. 
105.     /// <summary>
106.     /// Delete an enrollment (unenroll)
107.     /// </summary>
108.     /// <param name="id">Enrollment ID</param>
109.     /// <returns>No content</returns>
110.     [HttpDelete("{id}")]
111.     [Authorize(Roles = "Admin,Student")]
112.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status204NoContent)]
113.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
114.     public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int id)
115.     {
116.         var result = await _enrollmentService.DeleteAsync(id);
117.         
118.         if (!result)
119.         {
120.             return NotFound(new { message = $"Enrollment with ID {id} not found" });
121.         }
122. 
123.         return NoContent();
124.     }
125. }
126. 
```

1. using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
2. using CourseManagementAPI.Models;
3. 
4. namespace CourseManagementAPI.Data;
5. 
6. public class ApplicationDbContext : DbContext
7. {
8.     public ApplicationDbContext(DbContextOptions<ApplicationDbContext> options)
9.         : base(options)
10.     {
11.     }
12. 
13.     public DbSet<User> Users { get; set; }
14.     public DbSet<Instructor> Instructors { get; set; }
15.     public DbSet<InstructorProfile> InstructorProfiles { get; set; }
16.     public DbSet<Student> Students { get; set; }
17.     public DbSet<Course> Courses { get; set; }
18.     public DbSet<Enrollment> Enrollments { get; set; }
19. 
20.     protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
21.     {
22.         base.OnModelCreating(modelBuilder);
23. 
24.         // User configuration
25.         modelBuilder.Entity<User>(entity =>
26.         {
27.             entity.HasIndex(u => u.Username).IsUnique();
28.             entity.HasIndex(u => u.Email).IsUnique();
29.         });
30. 
31.         // Instructor configuration
32.         modelBuilder.Entity<Instructor>(entity =>
33.         {
34.             entity.HasIndex(i => i.Email).IsUnique();
35.         });
36. 
37.         // Student configuration
38.         modelBuilder.Entity<Student>(entity =>
39.         {
40.             entity.HasIndex(s => s.Email).IsUnique();
41.             entity.HasIndex(s => s.StudentNumber).IsUnique();
42.         });
43. 
44.         // Course configuration
45.         modelBuilder.Entity<Course>(entity =>
46.         {
47.             entity.HasIndex(c => c.CourseCode).IsUnique();
48.         });
49. 
50.         // One-to-One: Instructor - InstructorProfile
51.         modelBuilder.Entity<InstructorProfile>()
52.             .HasOne(p => p.Instructor)
53.             .WithOne(i => i.Profile)
54.             .HasForeignKey<InstructorProfile>(p => p.InstructorId)
55.             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
56. 
57.         // One-to-Many: Instructor - Courses
58.         modelBuilder.Entity<Course>()
59.             .HasOne(c => c.Instructor)
60.             .WithMany(i => i.Courses)
61.             .HasForeignKey(c => c.InstructorId)
62.             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);
63. 
64.         // Many-to-Many: Student - Course through Enrollment
65.         modelBuilder.Entity<Enrollment>()
66.             .HasOne(e => e.Student)
67.             .WithMany(s => s.Enrollments)
68.             .HasForeignKey(e => e.StudentId)
69.             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
70. 
71.         modelBuilder.Entity<Enrollment>()
72.             .HasOne(e => e.Course)
73.             .WithMany(c => c.Enrollments)
74.             .HasForeignKey(e => e.CourseId)
75.             .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
76. 
77.         // Unique constraint for Enrollment (prevent duplicate enrollments)
78.         modelBuilder.Entity<Enrollment>()
79.             .HasIndex(e => new { e.StudentId, e.CourseId })
80.             .IsUnique();
81. 
82.         // Configure decimal precision for Grade
83.         modelBuilder.Entity<Enrollment>()
84.             .Property(e => e.Grade)
85.             .HasPrecision(5, 2);
86. 
87.         // Seed data for Admin user
88.         modelBuilder.Entity<User>().HasData(
89.             new User
90.             {
91.                 Id = 1,
92.                 Username = "admin",
93.                 Email = "admin@coursemanagement.com",
94.                 PasswordHash = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.HashPassword("Admin@123"),
95.                 Role = "Admin",
96.                 CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow
97.             }
98.         );
99.     }
100. }
101.
```

The 400 error means the request body is missing required fields. Use this exact JSON:

```json
{
  "firstName": "John",
  "lastName": "Smith",
  "email": "john.smith@university.edu",
  "phone": "+1234567890",
  "department": "Computer Science"
}
```

- `firstName` (min 2 chars)
- `lastName` (min 2 chars)
- `email` (valid email format)
- `department` (required)

Make sure you:
1. Delete the default/example text in the request body
2. Paste the JSON above exactly
3. Click Execute

<details>
<summary>50 lines</summary>

```
1. # Course Management System API
2. 
3. A RESTful Web API built with ASP.NET Core for managing courses, instructors, students, and enrollments. This API implements JWT authentication, role-based authorization, and follows best practices for entity relationships and data management.
4. 
5. ## Table of Contents
6. 
7. - [Technologies Used](#technologies-used)
8. - [Features](#features)
9. - [Getting Started](#getting-started)
10. - [API Endpoints](#api-endpoints)
11. - [Authentication](#authentication)
12. - [Authorization](#authorization)
13. - [Database Schema](#database-schema)
14. - [HTTP-only Cookies for Authentication Security](#http-only-cookies-for-authentication-security)
15. 
16. ## Technologies Used
17. 
18. ### Core Framework
19. - **ASP.NET Core 7.0** - A cross-platform, high-performance framework for building modern cloud-based web applications. It provides the foundation for building RESTful APIs with built-in support for dependency injection, middleware, and routing.
20. 
21. ### ORM & Database
22. - **Entity Framework Core 7.0** - A modern object-relational mapper (ORM) that enables .NET developers to work with databases using .NET objects. It eliminates the need for most data-access code and provides features like LINQ queries, change tracking, and migrations.
23. 
24. - **SQLite** - A lightweight, serverless, self-contained SQL database engine. Perfect for development and small-scale deployments, requiring no separate database server installation.
25. 
26. ### Authentication & Security
27. - **JWT (JSON Web Tokens)** - An open standard (RFC 7519) for securely transmitting information between parties as a JSON object. Used for stateless authentication where the server doesn't need to store session information.
28. 
29. - **BCrypt.Net** - A password hashing library that implements the bcrypt algorithm. It provides secure one-way hashing with automatic salt generation, protecting user passwords even if the database is compromised.
30. 
31. ### API Documentation
32. - **Swagger/OpenAPI (Swashbuckle)** - An open-source framework for documenting RESTful APIs. It generates interactive documentation that allows developers to test endpoints directly from the browser.
33. 
34. ### Design Patterns & Principles
35. - **Dependency Injection** - Built-in IoC (Inversion of Control) container for managing service lifetimes and dependencies.
36. - **Repository/Service Pattern** - Clean separation between data access and business logic.
37. - **DTOs (Data Transfer Objects)** - Objects used to transfer data between layers, preventing exposure of entity models.
38. 
39. ## Features
40. 
41. - ✅ **Entity Relationships**
42.   - One-to-One: Instructor ↔ InstructorProfile
43.   - One-to-Many: Instructor → Courses
44.   - Many-to-Many: Student ↔ Course (via Enrollment)
45. 
46. - ✅ **JWT Authentication** - Secure token-based authentication
47. - ✅ **Role-based Authorization** - Admin, Instructor, Student roles
48. - ✅ **DTO Validation** - Request validation using Data Annotations
49. - ✅ **LINQ Optimizations** - Select() projections for efficient queries
50. - ✅ **AsNoTracking()** - Optimized read-only queries
```

</details>

1. using System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt;
2. using System.Security.Claims;
3. using System.Text;
4. using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
5. using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
6. using CourseManagementAPI.Data;
7. using CourseManagementAPI.DTOs;
8. using CourseManagementAPI.Models;
9. 
10. namespace CourseManagementAPI.Services;
11. 
12. public interface IAuthService
13. {
14.     Task<LoginResponseDto?> LoginAsync(LoginDto dto);
15.     Task<UserReadDto?> RegisterAsync(UserCreateDto dto);
16.     Task<UserReadDto?> GetUserByIdAsync(int id);
17. }
18. 
19. public class AuthService : IAuthService
20. {
21.     private readonly ApplicationDbContext _context;
22.     private readonly IConfiguration _configuration;
23. 
24.     public AuthService(ApplicationDbContext context, IConfiguration configuration)
25.     {
26.         _context = context;
27.         _configuration = configuration;
28.     }
29. 
30.     public async Task<LoginResponseDto?> LoginAsync(LoginDto dto)
31.     {
32.         var user = await _context.Users
33.             .FirstOrDefaultAsync(u => u.Username == dto.Username);
34. 
35.         if (user == null || !BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.Verify(dto.Password, user.PasswordHash))
36.         {
37.             return null;
38.         }
39. 
40.         // Update last login
41.         user.LastLoginAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
42.         await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
43. 
44.         var token = GenerateJwtToken(user);
45.         var expiration = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(
46.             double.Parse(_configuration["Jwt:ExpirationHours"] ?? "24"));
47. 
48.         return new LoginResponseDto
49.         {
50.             Token = token,
51.             Expiration = expiration,
52.             User = new UserReadDto
53.             {
54.                 Id = user.Id,
55.                 Username = user.Username,
56.                 Email = user.Email,
57.                 Role = user.Role,
58.                 CreatedAt = user.CreatedAt,
59.                 LastLoginAt = user.LastLoginAt
60.             }
61.         };
62.     }
63. 
64.     public async Task<UserReadDto?> RegisterAsync(UserCreateDto dto)
65.     {
66.         // Check if username or email already exists
67.         var exists = await _context.Users
68.             .AnyAsync(u => u.Username == dto.Username || u.Email == dto.Email);
69.         
70.         if (exists) return null;
71. 
72.         var user = new User
73.         {
74.             Username = dto.Username,
75.             Email = dto.Email,
76.             PasswordHash = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.HashPassword(dto.Password),
77.             Role = dto.Role,
78.             CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow
79.         };
80. 
81.         _context.Users.Add(user);
82.         await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
83. 
84.         return new UserReadDto
85.         {
86.             Id = user.Id,
87.             Username = user.Username,
88.             Email = user.Email,
89.             Role = user.Role,
90.             CreatedAt = user.CreatedAt
91.         };
92.     }
93. 
94.     public async Task<UserReadDto?> GetUserByIdAsync(int id)
95.     {
96.         return await _context.Users
97.             .AsNoTracking()
98.             .Where(u => u.Id == id)
99.             .Select(u => new UserReadDto
100.             {
101.                 Id = u.Id,
102.                 Username = u.Username,
103.                 Email = u.Email,
104.                 Role = u.Role,
105.                 CreatedAt = u.CreatedAt,
106.                 LastLoginAt = u.LastLoginAt
107.             })
108.             .FirstOrDefaultAsync();
109.     }
110. 
111.     private string GenerateJwtToken(User user)
112.     {
113.         var jwtKey = _configuration["Jwt:Key"] ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("JWT Key not configured");
114.         var key = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(jwtKey));
115.         var credentials = new SigningCredentials(key, SecurityAlgorithms.HmacSha256);
116. 
117.         var claims = new[]
118.         {
119.             new Claim(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier, user.Id.ToString()),
120.             new Claim(ClaimTypes.Name, user.Username),
121.             new Claim(ClaimTypes.Email, user.Email),
122.             new Claim(ClaimTypes.Role, user.Role)
123.         };
124. 
125.         var token = new JwtSecurityToken(
126.             issuer: _configuration["Jwt:Issuer"],
127.             audience: _configuration["Jwt:Audience"],
128.             claims: claims,
129.             expires: DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(
130.                 double.Parse(_configuration["Jwt:ExpirationHours"] ?? "24")),
131.             signingCredentials: credentials
132.         );
133. 
134.         return new JwtSecurityTokenHandler().WriteToken(token);
135.     }
136. }
137.
```

</details>

1. using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
2. using CourseManagementAPI.DTOs;
3. using CourseManagementAPI.Services;
4. 
5. namespace CourseManagementAPI.Controllers;
6. 
7. [ApiController]
8. [Route("api/[controller]")]
9. [Produces("application/json")]
10. public class AuthController : ControllerBase
11. {
12.     private readonly IAuthService _authService;
13. 
14.     public AuthController(IAuthService authService)
15.     {
16.         _authService = authService;
17.     }
18. 
19.     /// <summary>
20.     /// Login with username and password
21.     /// </summary>
22.     /// <param name="dto">Login credentials</param>
23.     /// <returns>JWT token and user info</returns>
24.     [HttpPost("login")]
25.     [ProducesResponseType(typeof(LoginResponseDto), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
26.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status401Unauthorized)]
27.     public async Task<ActionResult<LoginResponseDto>> Login([FromBody] LoginDto dto)
28.     {
29.         var result = await _authService.LoginAsync(dto);
30.         
31.         if (result == null)
32.         {
33.             return Unauthorized(new { message = "Invalid username or password" });
34.         }
35. 
36.         return Ok(result);
37.     }
38. 
39.     /// <summary>
40.     /// Register a new user
41.     /// </summary>
42.     /// <param name="dto">User registration data</param>
43.     /// <returns>Created user info</returns>
44.     [HttpPost("register")]
45.     [ProducesResponseType(typeof(UserReadDto), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
46.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status400BadRequest)]
47.     [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
48.     public async Task<ActionResult<UserReadDto>> Register([FromBody] UserCreateDto dto)
49.     {
50.         var result = await _authService.RegisterAsync(dto);
51.         
52.         if (result == null)
53.         {
54.             return Conflict(new { message = "Username or email already exists" });
55.         }
56. 
57.         return CreatedAtAction(nameof(Register), new { id = result.Id }, result);
58.     }
59. }
60. 
```

1. using System.Text;
2. using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer;
3. using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
4. using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
5. using Microsoft.OpenApi.Models;
6. using CourseManagementAPI.Data;
7. using CourseManagementAPI.Services;
8. 
9. var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);
10. 
11. // Add services to the container.
12. 
13. // Database Context - Using SQLite for easier setup
14. builder.Services.AddDbContext<ApplicationDbContext>(options =>
15.     options.UseSqlite(builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("DefaultConnection")));
16. 
17. // Register services with dependency injection
18. builder.Services.AddScoped<IAuthService, AuthService>();
19. builder.Services.AddScoped<IInstructorService, InstructorService>();
20. builder.Services.AddScoped<IStudentService, StudentService>();
21. builder.Services.AddScoped<ICourseService, CourseService>();
22. builder.Services.AddScoped<IEnrollmentService, EnrollmentService>();
23. 
24. // JWT Authentication
25. var jwtKey = builder.Configuration["Jwt:Key"] ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("JWT Key not configured");
26. builder.Services.AddAuthentication(options =>
27. {
28.     options.DefaultAuthenticateScheme = JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme;
29.     options.DefaultChallengeScheme = JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme;
30. })
31. .AddJwtBearer(options =>
32. {
33.     options.TokenValidationParameters = new TokenValidationParameters
34.     {
35.         ValidateIssuer = true,
36.         ValidateAudience = true,
37.         ValidateLifetime = true,
38.         ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,
39.         ValidIssuer = builder.Configuration["Jwt:Issuer"],
40.         ValidAudience = builder.Configuration["Jwt:Audience"],
41.         IssuerSigningKey = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(jwtKey))
42.     };
43. });
44. 
45. builder.Services.AddAuthorization();
46. 
47. builder.Services.AddControllers();
48. 
49. // Swagger/OpenAPI configuration
50. builder.Services.AddEndpointsApiExplorer();
51. builder.Services.AddSwaggerGen(options =>
52. {
53.     options.SwaggerDoc("v1", new OpenApiInfo
54.     {
55.         Title = "Course Management API",
56.         Version = "v1",
57.         Description = "ASP.NET Core Web API for managing courses, instructors, students, and enrollments",
58.         Contact = new OpenApiContact
59.         {
60.             Name = "Course Management Team"
61.         }
62.     });
63. 
64.     // Add JWT authentication to Swagger
65.     options.AddSecurityDefinition("Bearer", new OpenApiSecurityScheme
66.     {
67.         Description = "JWT Authorization header using the Bearer scheme. Enter 'Bearer' [space] and then your token.",
68.         Name = "Authorization",
69.         In = ParameterLocation.Header,
70.         Type = SecuritySchemeType.ApiKey,
71.         Scheme = "Bearer"
72.     });
73. 
74.     options.AddSecurityRequirement(new OpenApiSecurityRequirement
75.     {
76.         {
77.             new OpenApiSecurityScheme
78.             {
79.                 Reference = new OpenApiReference
80.                 {
81.                     Type = ReferenceType.SecurityScheme,
82.                     Id = "Bearer"
83.                 }
84.             },
85.             Array.Empty<string>()
86.         }
87.     });
88. });
89. 
90. var app = builder.Build();
91. 
92. // Configure the HTTP request pipeline.
93. if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
94. {
95.     app.UseSwagger();
96.     app.UseSwaggerUI(options =>
97.     {
98.         options.SwaggerEndpoint("/swagger/v1/swagger.json", "Course Management API v1");
99.     });
100. }
101. 
102. app.UseAuthentication();
103. app.UseAuthorization();
104. 
105. app.MapControllers();
106. 
107. // Apply migrations automatically in development
108. using (var scope = app.Services.CreateScope())
109. {
110.     var context = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<ApplicationDbContext>();
111.     context.Database.Migrate();
112. }
113. 
114. app.Run();
115.

39. 
40. ## Features
41. 
42. - ✅ **Entity Relationships**
43.   - One-to-One: Instructor ↔ InstructorProfile
44.   - One-to-Many: Instructor → Courses
45.   - Many-to-Many: Student ↔ Course (via Enrollment)
46. 
47. - ✅ **JWT Authentication** - Secure token-based authentication
48. - ✅ **Role-based Authorization** - Admin, Instructor, Student roles
49. - ✅ **DTO Validation** - Request validation using Data Annotations
50. - ✅ **LINQ Optimizations** - Select() projections for efficient queries
51. - ✅ **AsNoTracking()** - Optimized read-only queries
52. - ✅ **Async Operations** - Non-blocking database calls
53. - ✅ **Swagger Documentation** - Interactive API documentation
54. 
55. ## Getting Started
56. 
57. ### Prerequisites
58. 
59. - [.NET 7.0 SDK](https://dotnet.microsoft.com/download/dotnet/7.0) or later
60. - Any code editor (Visual Studio, VS Code, Rider)
```

</details>

104. ## API Endpoints
105. 
106. ### Authentication
107. | Method | Endpoint | Description | Auth Required |
108. |--------|----------|-------------|---------------|
109. | POST | `/api/auth/login` | Login and get JWT token | No |
110. | POST | `/api/auth/register` | Register new user | No |
111. 
112. ### Instructors
113. | Method | Endpoint | Description | Auth Required | Roles |
114. |--------|----------|-------------|---------------|-------|
115. | GET | `/api/instructors` | Get all instructors | Yes | Any |
116. | GET | `/api/instructors/{id}` | Get instructor by ID | Yes | Any |
117. | POST | `/api/instructors` | Create instructor | Yes | Admin |
118. | PUT | `/api/instructors/{id}` | Update instructor | Yes | Admin, Instructor |
119. | DELETE | `/api/instructors/{id}` | Delete instructor | Yes | Admin |
120. | GET | `/api/instructors/{id}/profile` | Get instructor profile | Yes | Any |

<sub>⏱️ 212m 44s</sub>

### 💬 Copilot

**Bonus features complete and pushed to GitHub!** ✅

Here's what I implemented:

### 1. **Refresh Tokens**
- New `RefreshToken` model with 7-day expiry
- `POST /api/auth/refresh-token` - Get new JWT using refresh token
- `POST /api/auth/revoke-token` - Invalidate a refresh token
- Login now returns both JWT and refresh token

### 2. **Hangfire Background Jobs**
- Dashboard at `/hangfire` (development mode)
- **3 recurring jobs:**
  - `cleanup-expired-tokens` - Daily at midnight
  - `daily-enrollment-report` - Daily at 8 AM
  - `auto-complete-enrollments` - Weekly

The project now fulfills all PDF requirements **plus** both bonus features. Your GitHub repo is updated at: https://github.com/AhmadElbaathy/CourseManagementAPI

40. ## Features
41. 
42. - ✅ **Entity Relationships**
43.   - One-to-One: Instructor ↔ InstructorProfile
44.   - One-to-Many: Instructor → Courses
45.   - Many-to-Many: Student ↔ Course (via Enrollment)
46. 
47. - ✅ **JWT Authentication** - Secure token-based authentication
48. - ✅ **Role-based Authorization** - Admin, Instructor, Student roles
49. - ✅ **DTO Validation** - Request validation using Data Annotations
50. - ✅ **LINQ Optimizations** - Select() projections for efficient queries
51. - ✅ **AsNoTracking()** - Optimized read-only queries
52. - ✅ **Async Operations** - Non-blocking database calls
53. - ✅ **Swagger Documentation** - Interactive API documentation
54. 
55. ### Bonus Features
56. - ✅ **Refresh Tokens** - Secure session renewal without re-login
57. - ✅ **Hangfire Background Jobs** - Scheduled tasks including:
58.   - Daily cleanup of expired refresh tokens
59.   - Daily enrollment statistics report
60.   - Weekly auto-completion of finished course enrollments
61. - ✅ **Hangfire Dashboard** - Monitor background jobs at `/hangfire`
62. 
63. ## Getting Started
64.

69. 
70. ### Installation
